## PKTAGINGTIMERr

- **Address:** `0x0d380000`  ·  **Size:** 4 bytes
- **Function:** DURATIONSELECT: 13 bits aging interval duration select registers.\nDuration select determines the interval at which the age timer\ngets incremented. After DURATIONSELECT number of clock timer\nticks (pulses) the age timer will be incremented by 1.\nPacket aging function is disabled when set this register to 0.\nPacket aging function is disabled by default after power-on reset.\nAGINGTICKSEL: Select Aging tick clock source.\n0: 125us clock pulse.\n1: 500ms clock pulse

## S3_WERRCOUNTr

- **Address:** `0x193000d0`  ·  **Size:** 4 bytes
- **Function:** The count in the bucket controlling WERR/WRR.\nNOTE: The addressing is based on the standard queue mapping in S3:\nfor ports 1..4:\naddress   0: MC0\naddress   1: UC0\naddress   2: EUC0\naddress   3: EUC1\n..       ..  ..\naddress  16: EUC14\naddress  17: EUC15\naddress  18: MC1\naddress  19: UC1\naddress  20: EUC16\n..       ..  ..\naddress  35: EUC23\n..\netc\nfor other ports:\naddress   0: MC0\naddress   1: UC0\naddress   2: MC1\naddress   3: UC1\naddress   4: MC2\naddress   5: UC2\naddress   6: MC3\naddress   7: UC3

**Fields:**

    | Bits | Field |
    |------|-------|
    | `[22:0]` | COUNT |
    | `[23]` | SIGN |
    | `[24]` | PARITY |

---

## S2_WERRCOUNTr

- **Address:** `0x1a3000d0`  ·  **Size:** 4 bytes
- **Function:** The count in the bucket controlling WERR/WRR.\nNOTE: The addressing is based on the standard queue indexing in S2:\naddress   0: S3.0\naddress   1: S3.1\naddress   2: S3.2\naddress   3: S3.3\naddress   4: UC0 (only available if S3_CONFIG.ROUTE_UC_TO_S2 is set)\naddress   5: UC1 (only available if S3_CONFIG.ROUTE_UC_TO_S2 is set)\naddress   6: UC2 (only available if S3_CONFIG.ROUTE_UC_TO_S2 is set)\naddress   7: UC3 (only available if S3_CONFIG.ROUTE_UC_TO_S2 is set)\naddress   8: UC4\naddress   9: UC5\naddr ...

**Fields:**

    | Bits | Field |
    |------|-------|
    | `[22:0]` | COUNT |
    | `[23]` | SIGN |
    | `[24]` | PARITY |

---

## S2_S3_ROUTINGr

- **Address:** `0x1a3000e0`  ·  **Size:** 8 bytes
- **Function:** The S3 group number routed to this S2 ETS group input, 0x1F means no connection\nNOTE: The addressing is based on the standard queue indexing in S2:\nS3 group number   0: S3.0\nS3 group number   1: S3.1\nS3 group number   2: S3.2\nS3 group number   3: S3.3\nS3 group number   4: UC0 (only available if S3_CONFIG.ROUTE_UC_TO_S2 is set)\nS3 group number   5: UC1 (only available if S3_CONFIG.ROUTE_UC_TO_S2 is set)\nS3 group number   6: UC2 (only available if S3_CONFIG.ROUTE_UC_TO_S2 is set)\nS3 group number   ...

**Fields:**

    | Bits | Field |
    |------|-------|
    | `[4:0]` | S3_GROUP_NO_I0 |
    | `[9:5]` | S3_GROUP_NO_I1 |
